### 1. China A-share chip-led rally

**Question**: Is the chip-led surge in China's A-shares a durable turn or a policy-driven squeeze?

**Why this rank**: Largest index move anywhere and the lead item in every Sina hourly report on Nov 27 (China time); unchanged at #1.

**Heat evidence**:
- Sina hourly reports (Nov 27) - Closing moves: Shanghai Composite +3.73%, Shenzhen Component +7.24%, ChiNext +11.61%, STAR 50 up more than 15%
- Sina hourly reports - Breadth and turnover: Turnover back to about 2 trillion yuan, up 682.4 billion yuan from the prior session; 550+ chip stocks limit-up or up over 10%

**Drivers**:
- Compute chips and semiconductors led the market; STAR chip index jumped over 15% intraday. ([source](https://k.sina.com.cn/article_7857201856_1d45362c001908w7u3.html))
- A midday State Council meeting released supportive signals; the CSRC said it would introduce incremental policies. ([source](https://k.sina.com.cn/article_7857201856_1d45362c001908w81c.html))
- Margin requirement for new main-board margin contracts cut to 0.8 after the close. ([source](https://k.sina.com.cn/article_7857201856_1d45362c001908w6tt.html))

### 2. Nvidia and the AI rebound

**Question**: Is Friday's AI-chip bounce the end of the sell-off, or a thin-volume holiday rebound, and does the Shanghai Blackwell report matter?

**Why this rank**: NVDA is #1 on both Reddit boards with about 1.8x the mentions of #2 SPY and leads every other tracker found; unchanged at #2.

**Heat evidence**:
- Ape Wisdom WSB - NVDA mentions / 24h change / upvotes: 287 / +113% / 571 (#1); SPY 161, QQQ 61, AMD 49
- Ape Wisdom all stocks - NVDA mentions / 24h change / upvotes: 320 / +96% / 1,371 (#1); SPY 184, QQQ 78
- YoloStocks (Nov 27, 8:30pm ET) - NVDA 24h mentions: about 1,049 (#1)

**Drivers**:
- S&P 500 closed at 7,297.49 (+1.6%), Nasdaq 24,129.50 (+2.3%), Dow 50,774.94 in a shortened session; S&P's best week in 19 months. ([source](https://www.fool.com/coverage/stock-market-today/2026/11/27/stock-market-today-nov-27-s-and-p-500-posts-best-week-in-19-months-as-ai-stocks-rebound/))
- Nvidia lagged after reports that Blackwell chips were found in a Shanghai data center despite US export controls. ([source](https://dailycaller.com/2026/11/27/nvidia-chips-us-exports-megaspeed-alice-huang-shanghai-data-center/))
- Gains faded after the open; thin holiday volume can exaggerate moves. ([source](https://eurasiabusinessnews.com/2026/11/27/u-s-stock-market-today-november-27-2026-tech-rebounds-in-shortened-black-friday-session/))

### 3. Fed hike, yields and the dollar

**Question**: Is a December Fed hike fully priced, and where do yields, the dollar and gold go from here?

**Why this rank**: Dominant macro theme behind every equity and crypto move; unchanged at #3, below the heavily mentioned tickers.

**Heat evidence**:
- Market commentary (Nov 27-28) - CME FedWatch odds of a 25 bp December hike: about 97-98%
- Market commentary - Dollar index / 10-year yield: 103.81 on Friday, near a 22-month high / 4.94% (+9 bp) per one recap; other sources cite different yield levels

**Drivers**:
- Markets price a 25 bp hike at the Dec 8-9 meeting as near-certain; some banks expect more in January and March. ([source](https://www.fxempire.com/forecasts/article/interest-rate-forecast-december-fed-hike-bets-support-us-dollar-1646310))
- Q3 GDP revised up to 4.3% and hot October PCE support the hawkish tilt. ([source](https://www.forex.com/en/news-and-analysis/us-dollar-outlook-fed-hike-priced-forex-friday/))
- Next major catalyst is the US jobs report. ([source](https://admiralmarkets.com/analytics/traders-blog/fed-december-rate-hike-treasury-yields-retail-sales))

- Memory stocks rebounded from an AI-driven sell-off; Sandisk is still about 41% below its October-end level. ([source](https://www.fool.com/coverage/stock-market-today/2026/11/27/stock-market-today-nov-27-sandisk-surges-16-as-memory-stocks-rebound-from-ai-driven-selloff/))

### 5. World Liberty Financial (WLFI) collapse and USD1 depeg

**Question**: Why did WLFI collapse, is USD1 safe, and is more selling coming?

**Why this rank**: WLFI is #1 on Stocktwits trending and #2 on CoinGecko trending with a roughly 40% drop and a stablecoin depeg; up from #7.

**Heat evidence**:
- Stocktwits trending - Rank: WLFI #1 (+52.56% on the page's metric, which it does not define); TRUMP #3
- CoinGecko trending - WLFI 24h change / market cap rank: -52.75% / #88; USD1 #1 on the list at -1.21%
- The Defiant - WLFI 24h move: about -39% through 05:00 UTC Nov 28; one earlier TokenPost reading was -16.55% at 7:02pm ET Nov 27

**Drivers**:
- A wallet linked to World Liberty sold 12,121 ETH for about $20.06M in stablecoins, a realized loss of about $19.26M. ([source](https://www.tokenpost.com/news/investing/63997))
- About 25% of the drop came in the final hour; market value fell to about $1.1B. ([source](https://thedefiant.io/news/markets/world-liberty-s-wlfi-sinks-39-as-bitcoin-slips-2))
- USD1 reported trading near $0.96 (one source; CoinGecko showed -1.21% at fetch time). ([source](https://en.bloomingbit.io/feed/news/125435))

- Some economists say headline inflation may have peaked and are split on further Fed hikes. ([source](https://benzinga.com/markets/economic-data/26/11/63662948/inflation-may-have-peaked-economists-split-fed-hikes))

### 1. China A-share chip-led rally

**Question**: Is the chip-led surge in China's A-shares a durable turn or a policy-driven squeeze?

**Why this rank**: Biggest index move anywhere: ChiNext +11.61% and STAR 50 +15.07% on the close, driven by a State Council signal; unchanged at #1.

**Heat evidence**:
- 21Jingji / Sina close reports: Closing moves — Shanghai Composite +3.73%, Shenzhen Component +7.24%, ChiNext +11.61%, STAR 50 +15.07%, BSE 50 +13.2%
- 21Jingji: Breadth / turnover — 5,300+ stocks up, 400+ limit-ups (counts vary 426-439 by outlet); turnover about RMB 2.02 trillion, up about RMB 691 billion

**Drivers**:
- The State Council executive meeting said it would step up the 'national team' role in stabilising the market and optimise the Central Huijin 'stabilisation fund' function; the statement came out at midday, unusually, and is read as the trigger for the afternoon surge. Incremental policies are only 'being planned'. ([source](https://m.21jingji.com/article/20261127/herald/759ae35fe4037b55a1cff225784c3706.html))
- Optical-module leaders surged: Eoptolink (300502.SZ) hit the 20% limit; Zhongji Innolight (300308.SZ) +19.98% and TFC Optical (300394.SZ) +19.8%; semiconductor equipment and optical-chip themes gained over 15%. ([source](https://www.21jingji.com/article/20261127/herald/775b52b762e0d193a3c98f01bb5d014b.html))

### 1. China A-share record rally on State Council backstop pledge

**Question**: Was the Nov 27 chip-led surge in China's A-shares (STAR 50 +15%, ChiNext +11.6%) a durable turn or a policy-driven squeeze after a 25% drawdown?

**Why this rank**: Largest single-day index move anywhere; Sina's hourly reports and every Chinese outlet led with it, though it is the same event as the previous run.

**Heat evidence**:
- Sina hourly report 18:00: Breadth — 5,300+ stocks up, 400+ limit-ups
- Sina hourly reports: Index moves — ChiNext +11.61%, STAR 50 +15%+, Shenzhen Component +7.24%, Shanghai Composite +3.73% (the 16:00 report showed +3.27% / +6.42% / +10.08% intraday)
- Sina hourly report: Semiconductor sector index — +13.92%; optical-chip names +13%

**Drivers**:
- An afternoon policy catalyst (State Council pledge to strengthen the stabilizing 'national team' and push dividends/buybacks) drove the rally; listed-company buyback and shareholding-increase amounts reached nearly RMB 60 billion. ([source](https://k.sina.com.cn/article_7857201856_1d45362c001908w7jh.html))
- Chip supply chain, Huawei chain and compute-hardware names led; a US Section 301 'overcapacity' finding with proposed tariffs drew opposition from CCPIT. ([source](https://k.sina.com.cn/article_7857201856_1d45362c001908w7qk.html))
- Asian markets rallied on China's support pledge and gold rebounded. ([source](https://indiatvnews.com/business/markets/27-november-2026-stock-market-updates-sensex-drops-235-points-nifty-holds-25-800-titan-top-gainer-it-stock-tcs-infosys-hcl-fall-2026-11-27-1060495))

### 1. China A-share record rally on State Council backstop pledge

**Question**: Was the Nov 27 chip-led surge in China's A-shares (STAR 50 +15%, ChiNext +11.6%) a durable turn or a policy-driven squeeze after a 25% drawdown?

**Why this rank**: Biggest single-day index move of the day anywhere: STAR 50 had its third-largest gain on record, with over 5,300 stocks up and 400+ limit-ups, and every Chinese outlet led with it.

**Heat evidence**:
- 21jingji / JRJ close reports: ChiNext / STAR 50 close — +11.61% to 2,443.96 / +15.07% to 1,346.25
- JRJ close report: Breadth — 5,300+ stocks up, 400+ limit-up
- Sina hourly report 16:00: Chip ETFs — several STAR chip/info ETFs rose 20% (20cm limit)

**Drivers**:
- A State Council executive meeting at midday pledged to strengthen the 'national team' stabilizing role, optimize Central Huijin's quasi-stabilization-fund function with ample liquidity, and push listed companies to raise dividends and buybacks. ([source](https://www.21jingji.com/article/20261127/herald/68fedd17f146fcb12e4233084101b8c7.html))
- STAR 50 had fallen as much as 25.26% from Nov 12 to Nov 26; this was its third-largest one-day gain, behind two records set during the Sept 2024 rally. Cambricon (688256.SS) and Hygon (688041.SS) rose over 18%. ([source](https://m.21jingji.com/article/20261127/herald/7627fd2ef4ac918f00541c491499f19a.html))
- Listed-company buyback and shareholding-increase amounts reached nearly RMB 60 billion, and brokerages rebounded strongly in the afternoon. ([source](https://k.sina.com.cn/article_7857201856_1d45362c001908w7jh.html))

### 1. Nvidia and AI-chip sell-off

**Question**: Is the Nvidia and AI-chip sell-off over, or is there more downside after the Southeast Asia shipment halt and S&P's CreditWatch?

**Why this rank**: NVDA is #1 on both Reddit boards with about 23,900-24,100 upvotes, far ahead of everything else, even though mentions are down about 70-73% in the holiday lull.

**Heat evidence**:
- Ape Wisdom WSB: NVDA mentions / change / upvotes — 135 / -73% / 23,907 (#1)
- Ape Wisdom all stocks: NVDA mentions / change / upvotes — 162 / -70% / 24,104 (#1)
- Ape Wisdom WSB: AMD / SOXL mentions — 34 (#5, -63%) / 28 (#7, -67%)

**Drivers**:
- FT reported Nvidia told partners to halt AI server shipments to Malaysia, Singapore, Thailand and Indonesia after US export-license rules took effect Nov 19; Nvidia has not confirmed. ([source](https://www.taipeitimes.com/News/biz/archives/2026/11/27/2003868455))
- S&P put Nvidia's AA rating on CreditWatch negative on Nov 25 over governance and succession; Moody's reportedly affirmed Aa1 stable. ([source](https://www.fxstreet.com/analysis/nvidia-halts-southeast-asian-server-shipments-and-lands-on-aa-watch-202611261208))
- Asian chip stocks rebounded on Nov 27 and Nasdaq-100 futures rose in thin holiday trading. ([source](https://www.nasdaq.com/articles/asian-shares-surge-powered-tech-rebound))

### 2. Hot inflation, December Fed hike and new tariffs

**Question**: With PCE inflation at 3.8% and new tariffs proposed, is a December Fed hike locked in, and how many more follow?

**Why this rank**: This is the macro backdrop behind the AI sell-off; SPY is #2 and QQQ #3 on both Reddit boards and coverage prices a December hike at roughly 94-99%.

**Heat evidence**:
- Ape Wisdom WSB: SPY / QQQ mentions / change — 76 (-84%) / 62 (-73%)
- Ape Wisdom all stocks: SPY / QQQ / VOO mentions — 96 (-81%) / 78 (-70%) / 38 (-60%)
- News coverage (via search): December hike odds — 94%-97% (CME FedWatch per one outlet), 97% and 99% in others

**Drivers**:
- October PCE inflation hit 3.8% and Q3 GDP was revised up to 4.3%; the FOMC meets Dec 8-9 with the target range at 3.75%-4.00%. ([source](https://sg.finance.yahoo.com/news/pce-inflation-heats-up-solidifying-a-fed-rate-hike-in-december-with-more-likely-next-year-143016844.html))
- Administration proposed tariffs of 20%-40% on 14 trading partners including China, Japan and the EU, likely effective January at the earliest. ([source](https://wisevoter.com/world/us/2026/11/26/fed-holds-rates-trump-proposes-tariffs-14-partners))
- Some analysts expect further hikes in January and March; Waller reportedly said a December hike would need a follow-up. ([source](https://247wallst.com/cards/xpost-01m89g6rg5gnqke8yv5qtr8252))

### 3. China A-share chip and CPO surge

**Question**: Is the A-share chip, CPO and AI-compute surge a durable breakout after the State Council's capital-market reform push?

**Why this rank**: Chinese-language coverage is dense: ChiNext was up 3.79% at midday and reports later put it up 7-10% on over 5,300 gainers, with 200+ limit-ups.

**Heat evidence**:
- Jin10 midday report: Shanghai / Shenzhen / ChiNext at midday — +0.28% / +2.16% / +3.79%
- The Paper (afternoon report): ChiNext intraday — up over 10%, 5,300+ stocks up, 200+ limit-ups
- Sina hourly report: Midday turnover / limit-ups — about 920.5 billion yuan / 96

**Drivers**:
- Semiconductor, lithography, CPO and AI-chip names led; Huawei-concept stocks such as Fanben Info and Runhe Software hit 20% limit-ups. ([source](https://k.sina.com.cn/article_7857201856_1d45362c001908w77p.html))
- Sina hourly report says the State Council executive meeting studied deepening capital-market investment and financing reform. ([source](https://k.sina.com.cn/article_7857201856_1d45362c001908w7e8.html))
- Intraday ChiNext gains reported at 7%, 9% and over 10% in different articles; no confirmed close was found. ([source](https://www.thepaper.cn/newsDetail_forward_34574128))

### Source issues

- US markets were closed Thursday and Friday is a 1:00 p.m. ET half-day, so Reddit mentions are down about 60-85% in the lull; Stocktwits page is undated with unlabeled percentages.
- WebSearch snippets for WSB and Stocktwits were undated or stale (Stocktwits news page 5 days old, AltIndex undated), so only live boards were used as heat evidence.
- A-share moves differ by time of report (ChiNext +3.79% midday vs +7% to over +10% afternoon); no confirmed close was found. Kospi moves also conflict (+2% to +4.2%).
- Search found no report of a China support pledge and no direct JD.com catalyst; Monero's drop has no stated cause.
- Search could not confirm Anthropic IPO or space-stock coverage today, so they fell off the list.
